## 2026-10-11 — TFT UI reference scan

Read-only UI reference notes for resonance presentation. Riot’s TFT interface article documents the PC level/XP/shop-odds/action grouping; Riot’s mobile update documents the toggle-open shop and dedicated item inventory; Set 13 QoL notes document the left item toolbar and 20-slot inventory. Riot patch notes document trait-tracker overflow and trait-related state display. For 金铲铲-specific screen examples, a 4399-hosted guide labeled as an official announcement includes screenshots and descriptions for the income breakdown marker, shop odds, item combining, and unit detail; the host is not Tencent, so treated as republished visual evidence rather than primary-host documentation. Tencent app ownership/listing cross-check: Apple App Store and Xiaomi listing. Recommendations to keep the active-trait summary compact, put full pact thresholds/details behind inspect, and expose reward progress only when actionable are design inferences, not claims about an official fixed layout.

References: https://teamfighttactics.leagueoflegends.com/en-gb/news/game-updates/tft-interface-update/ ; https://teamfighttactics.leagueoflegends.com/en-gb/news/riot-games/teamfight-tactics-mobile-update/ ; https://teamfighttactics.leagueoflegends.com/en-gb/news/dev/dev-quality-of-life-improvements/ ; https://teamfighttactics.leagueoflegends.com/en-gb/news/game-updates/teamfight-tactics-patch-15-1-notes-2025/ ; https://teamfighttactics.leagueoflegends.com/en-gb/news/game-updates/teamfight-tactics-patch-14-9-notes/ ; https://bbs.4399.cn/thread-view-tid-41755859 ; https://apps.apple.com/cn/app/%E9%87%91%E9%93%B2%E9%93%B2%E4%B9%8B%E6%88%98/id1478101301 ; https://app.mi.com/details?id=com.tencent.jkchess

## 2026-10-11 Native UI entry audit (read-only)
- Default live session routes to `NativeMatchConnection` (`public/js/resonance/app.js:199-200`); the former `GameScreen`/`GameChrome`/`InventoryPanel` path (`public/js/resonance/app.js:199`, `public/js/resonance/gameChrome.js:79,104,114-116`, `public/js/resonance/panels.js:59-69`) is selected only when replayMode is true (`app.js:81,145`), so its legacy equipment panel is not the current live UI.
- Native top bar (`public/js/resonance/native-match-screen.js:1792-1798`, `public/js/ui/hud.js:335-375`): exit opens local exit flow; player/enemy check buttons open Native drawers; round/phase/LP/countdown are read-only; pause delegates `g.pause`; Ready is shown only in PREP and delegates Native `g.ready`. The server accepts ready/pause and validates phase (`server/modes/resonance/native-match.js:281-309`). No unsupported action found in these controls.
- Native left edge has a vertical loose-item rail (`native-match-screen.js:1778-1780`) and `gm__bonds` strip (`:1800-1819`); battle and prep share the strip. The bottom-left `gm__corner` nav (`:1856-1862`) opens pact matrix, income, settlement, equipment. Pacts strip and pact matrix intentionally overlap information (quick active tier/count vs full matrix); gold quick-read in ShopBar and income panel overlap at the summary/detail level. Equipment is the clear duplicate: the rail opener and corner “装备库存” open the same panel (`:1778-1780,1861,1880-1883`), and a second InventoryDock is mounted in that panel while the rail remains mounted. Equipment combine/equip uses dock callbacks and session action path (`native/equipmentDock.js:39-45,57-75`; native screen `:1780,1882-1883`), not a dead panel.
- ShopBar is shown in PREP/SP_DRAFT/ROUND_START (`native-match-screen.js:478-480,1833-1841`); item cards are suppressed for Resonance (`hideItem`), while freeze/refresh/XP/buy are wired to `g.freeze/g.refresh/g.levelUp/g.buy` (`:1833-1837`). The mode maps freeze→lockShop, refresh→refresh, levelUp→buyXp and buys the selected offer (`server/modes/resonance/native-match.js:245-258`). Its funds/interest controls open the income panel (`native-match-screen.js:111`); this is a redundant route, not an unsupported action. Combat switches to CombatHud; the shop is absent, while corner read-only panels remain available.
- Dead control found: Native `EmoteWheel` is displayed and calls `actions.emote` (`native-match-screen.js:1863`; `public/js/ui/gameActions.js:13,69`), which sends `g.emote`; protocol accepts its shape (`shared/protocol.js:475`) but `ResonanceMatch.handle` has no `g.emote` branch and rejects unknown `g.*` as BAD_MSG (`server/modes/resonance/native-match.js:311`). Recommendation: hide/disable EmoteWheel for Resonance or add deliberate server-mode support.
- Icon coverage is structurally present: pact icons resolve direct/native/explicit/semantic/faction/role assets with role glyph fallback (`public/js/resonance/pactIcon.js:61-98,101-108`); item icons use GIcon fallback (`public/js/resonance/itemIcon.js:17-23`); ShopBar lock/refresh images have snow/refresh fallback (`public/js/ui/shopBar.js:318-325`). The fullscreen-new-tab control is a text arrow rather than an icon (`native-match-screen.js:1864`), but has an accessible label/title; a separate actual FullscreenButton is also present (`:1867`), so fullscreen is duplicated.
- No black-on-black rule found in inspected surfaces: Native theme sets body text to `#f2f2f2` (`public/css/resonance-native/theme.css:51-55,146-155`); corner buttons and equipment rail use `var(--text-hi)` on dark backgrounds (`public/css/resonance.css:169-190`). One CSS inconsistency: Native pact/result details reference undefined `--text-mid`/`--text-low` while the theme defines `--text-md`/`--text-lo` (`resonance.css:196,202,204-205,282,286`; `theme.css:52-55`). Those invalid color declarations inherit the light parent color in the inspected selectors; this is not evidence of black text, but muted text contrast is unintended/less controlled. No browser was started, so this is source-level evidence only.
- Evidence sources inspected read-only: `public/js/resonance/app.js`, `native-match-screen.js`, `native/equipmentDock.js`, `gameChrome.js`, `panels.js`, `pactIcon.js`, `itemIcon.js`; `public/js/ui/hud.js`, `shopBar.js`, `gameActions.js`; `server/modes/resonance/native-match.js`, `shared/protocol.js`; `public/css/resonance.css`, `public/css/resonance-native/theme.css`.
- 2026-10-11：新增 V3 远征盟约展示测试 `E:/WindowsWorkspace/webworks/apps/stronghold-protocol/.worktrees/resonance/stronghold-protocol/test/resonance/pact-presentation.test.js`。测试以显式 canonical `data/resonance-native` 输入构建真实 rulesVersion=3 catalog 与 display DTO，确认 `TFT13_Warband` 的 `knockout_treasure` 全档阈值为 10/35/65/100/150/200/330/440/550/660/750/850/1000/1200/1500，UI 缩写只替换重复奖箱句、其它档位效果文案不变；当前/待领取/已领下一档/全部已领进度正确，展示 DTO 数组可变但不反写 session。`node --test test/resonance/pact-presentation.test.js`：3/3通过、0失败、0跳过，退出0；SP_DATA_DIR及三个独立设计路径均指向 canonical `E:/WindowsWorkspace/webworks/apps/stronghold-protocol/data/resonance-native`。另更正前一轮只读UI盘点：`GIcon`/`Icon` 的 `bond`、`item`、`report` 名称未注册且组件无通用替代，故不能据 `pactIconUrl` 或 item artwork fallback 推断这些角标/打开按钮有图标；受影响位置含 `public/js/resonance/native-match-screen.js:1858-1861` 和 `public/js/resonance/native/equipmentDock.js:75`。本轮未改生产源码/启动浏览器。
